# Schema do Top Gerente - dump de referência

> Conteúdo convertido para Markdown para consulta. **Não executar este arquivo no banco do cliente.** A integração deve usar apenas SELECT e somente nas tabelas necessárias.

```sql
CREATE TABLE `abastecimentos` (
  `id` int(11) NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `bomba` tinyint(2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `bico` tinyint(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `data_hora` datetime NOT NULL DEFAULT '0000-00-00 00:00:00',
  `quantidade` double(8,3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.000',
  `vlr_unitario` double(6,3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.000',
  `vlr_total` double(8,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.00',
  `encerrante_ml` double(12,3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.000',
  `encerrante_vlr` double(12,3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.000',
  `no_ecf` tinyint(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `serie` char(3) DEFAULT NULL,
  `no_cupom` int(9) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `no_item` smallint(4) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `status` char(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDENTE',
  `tanque` tinyint(2) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `encerrante_inic` double(12,3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.000',
  `bomba_empresa` tinyint(2) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `numero_serie` char(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `encerrante` (`bomba`,`bico`,`encerrante_ml`),
  KEY `no_ecf` (`no_ecf`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=latin1;
CREATE TABLE `abastecimentos_exc` (
  `id` int(11) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `bomba` tinyint(2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `bico` tinyint(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `data_hora` datetime NOT NULL DEFAULT '0000-00-00 00:00:00',
  `quantidade` double(8,3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.000',
  `vlr_unitario` double(6,3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.000',
  `vlr_total` double(8,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.00',
  `encerrante_ml` double(12,3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.000',
  `encerrante_vlr` double(12,3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.000',
  `no_ecf` tinyint(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `serie` char(3) DEFAULT NULL,
  `no_cupom` int(9) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `no_item` smallint(4) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `status` char(10) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDENTE',
  `tanque` tinyint(2) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `encerrante_inic` double(12,3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.000',
  `bomba_empresa` tinyint(2) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `numero_serie` char(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT ''
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=latin1;
CREATE TABLE `abastecimentos_manuais` (
  `Id` int(11) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `Bomba` smallint(2) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `Bico` smallint(3) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `Data_Movimento` datetime NOT NULL DEFAULT '0000-00-00 00:00:00',
  `Quantidade` double(8,3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.000',
  `Vlr_Unitario` double(6,3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.000',
  `Vlr_Total` double(8,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.00',
  `Encerrante_Final` double(12,3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.000',
  `No_Ecf` smallint(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `No_Cupom` int(10) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `Serie` char(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `No_Item` smallint(4) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `status` char(10) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `Id_Abastecimentos` int(11) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  PRIMARY KEY (`Id`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=latin1;
CREATE TABLE `ajuste_fluxo` (
  `empresa` smallint(3) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `data` date NOT NULL DEFAULT '0000-00-00',
  `sub_sist` char(2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `seq` tinyint(2) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `descricao` char(100) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `valor` double(9,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.00',
  PRIMARY KEY (`empresa`,`data`,`sub_sist`,`seq`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=latin1;
CREATE TABLE `aplicativo` (
  `Sigla` char(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `Nome` varchar(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  PRIMARY KEY (`Sigla`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=latin1;
CREATE TABLE `apur_guias` (
  `emp` tinyint(2) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `dt_apur` date NOT NULL DEFAULT '0000-00-00',
  `tp_imp` tinyint(1) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `tp_guia` char(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `uf` char(2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `seq` tinyint(2) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `num_guia` char(15) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `dt` date DEFAULT NULL,
  `vl` double(8,2) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.00',
  `org_loc` char(40) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  PRIMARY KEY (`emp`,`dt_apur`,`tp_imp`,`tp_guia`,`uf`,`seq`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=latin1;
CREATE TABLE `apuracao` (
  `emp` tinyint(2) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `dt` date NOT NULL DEFAULT '0000-00-00',
  `tp_imp` tinyint(1) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `uf` char(2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `linha_apur` tinyint(2) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `seq` tinyint(2) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `descr` char(50) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `vl` double(12,2) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.00',
  PRIMARY KEY (`emp`,`dt`,`tp_imp`,`uf`,`linha_apur`,`seq`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=latin1;
CREATE TABLE `arquivo` (
  `aplic` char(15) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `arq_pasta` char(200) NOT NULL DEFAULT ''
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=latin1;
CREATE TABLE `atc_def_tab_preco` (
  `tabela` tinyint(2) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `descr` char(10) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `tab_base` tinyint(2) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `pct_tab_base` float(6,3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.000',
  PRIMARY KEY (`tabela`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=latin1;
CREATE TABLE `atc_tmp_tab_preco` (
  `codigo` char(20) NOT NULL,
  `seq` char(1) NOT NULL,
  `produto` char(50) NOT NULL,
  `unidade` char(3) NOT NULL,
  `fator` double(6,3) NOT NULL,
  `tab_1` double(8,2) NOT NULL,
  `tab_2` double(8,2) NOT NULL,
  `tab_3` double(8,2) NOT NULL,
  `tab_4` double(8,2) NOT NULL,
  `tab_5` double(8,2) NOT NULL,
  `Suprime` char(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'N',
  PRIMARY KEY (`codigo`,`seq`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=latin1;
CREATE TABLE `atprecobombafis` (
  `id` tinyint(1) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `at_preco_bombaf` tinyint(1) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=latin1;
CREATE TABLE `atualiza` (
  `ControleG` bigint(11) NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `Comando` text NOT NULL,
  `Cliente1` tinyint(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `Cliente2` tinyint(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `Cliente3` tinyint(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `Cliente4` tinyint(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `Cliente5` tinyint(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  PRIMARY KEY (`ControleG`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB AUTO_INCREMENT=310 DEFAULT CHARSET=latin1;
CREATE TABLE `buf_colaborador` (
  `codigo` mediumint(5) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `nome_abreviado` char(15) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `nome` char(50) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `cpf_cnpj` char(14) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `rg` char(15) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `endereco` char(40) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `numero` mediumint(6) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `complemento` char(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `bairro` char(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `cep` char(8) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `municipio` int(7) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `ddd` tinyint(2) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `telefone` int(9) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `dt_nascimento` date DEFAULT NULL,
  `ctps` char(25) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `dt_admissao` date DEFAULT NULL,
  `funcao` smallint(3) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `fornecedor` mediumint(5) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `filiacao_pai` char(50) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `filiacao_mae` char(50) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `observacao` text,
  `ativo` char(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'S',
  `id_cadast` char(19) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `id_atual` char(19) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  PRIMARY KEY (`codigo`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=latin1;
CREATE TABLE `buf_estrut_itens` (
  `nivel1` smallint(3) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `nivel2` smallint(3) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `nivel3` smallint(3) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `produto` char(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `cons_medio_pes` float(6,3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.000',
  `cons_med_p_red` float(6,3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.000',
  PRIMARY KEY (`nivel1`,`nivel2`,`nivel3`,`produto`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=latin1;
CREATE TABLE `buf_evento` (
  `codigo` mediumint(5) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `nome` char(40) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `descricao` char(100) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `data` date DEFAULT NULL,
  `hora` char(5) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `local` smallint(3) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `qtd_colaborador` smallint(3) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `remun_padrao` float(6,2) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.00',
  `id_cadast` char(19) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `id_atual` char(19) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  PRIMARY KEY (`codigo`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=latin1;
CREATE TABLE `buf_evento_colab` (
  `evento` mediumint(5) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `colaborador` mediumint(5) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `remuneracao` float(6,2) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.00',
  `confirmado` char(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'N',
  `dt_confirmacao` date DEFAULT NULL,
  `compareceu` char(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'N',
  `observacao` char(50) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `id_cadast` char(19) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `id_atual` char(19) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  PRIMARY KEY (`evento`,`colaborador`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=latin1;
CREATE TABLE `buf_funcao` (
  `codigo` smallint(3) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `descricao` char(40) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `fixo` char(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'N',
  `ativo` char(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'S',
  `id_cadast` char(19) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `id_atual` char(19) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `operacao_func` smallint(6) NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`codigo`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=latin1;
CREATE TABLE `buf_local_eventos` (
  `codigo` smallint(3) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `nome` char(40) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `endereco` char(40) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `numero` mediumint(6) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `bairro` char(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `complemento` char(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `cep` int(8) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `municipio` int(7) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `ddd` tinyint(2) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `telefone` int(9) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `contato` char(40) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `ativo` char(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'S',
  `id_cadast` char(19) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `id_atual` char(19) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `descr_municipio` char(40) DEFAULT NULL,
  `UF` char(2) DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`codigo`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=latin1;
CREATE TABLE `buf_orc` (
  `emp` smallint(3) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `orc` mediumint(6) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `dt_orc` date DEFAULT NULL,
  `cliente` mediumint(5) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `nome` char(40) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `status` char(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `evento` mediumint(5) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `duracao` char(5) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `tipo` smallint(3) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `qtde_pes` mediumint(5) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `qtde_pes_red` mediumint(5) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `taxa_adm` float(5,2) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.00',
  `qtde_garc` smallint(3) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `tab_preco_buf` tinyint(2) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `tab_preco_alug` tinyint(2) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `tab_preco_decor` tinyint(2) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `vlr_custo_pes` float(7,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.00',
  `vlr_desc_pes` float(7,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.00',
  `vlr_acresc_pes` float(7,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.00',
  `vlr_adm_pes` float(7,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.00',
  `vlr_pes` float(7,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.00',
  `vlr_custo_p_red` float(7,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.00',
  `vlr_desc_p_red` float(7,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.00',
  `vlr_acres_p_red` float(7,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.00',
  `vlr_adm_pes_red` float(7,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.00',
  `vlr_pes_red` float(7,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.00',
  `vlr_desc_tot` double(11,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.00',
  `vlr_acresc_tot` double(11,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.00',
  `vlr_tot` double(11,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.00',
  `vlr_desc_global` double(11,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.00',
  `vlr_liquido` double(11,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.00',
  `cod_cond_pagto` tinyint(2) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `id_cadast` char(19) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `id_atual` char(19) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  PRIMARY KEY (`emp`,`orc`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=latin1;
CREATE TABLE `buf_orc_itens` (
  `emp` smallint(3) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `orc` int(9) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `nivel1` smallint(3) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `nivel2` smallint(3) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `nivel3` smallint(3) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `produto` char(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `cons_pes` float(6,3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.000',
  `cons_pes_red` float(6,3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.000',
  `qtde_total` double(12,3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.000',
  `vlr_unit` double(11,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.00',
  `vlr_total` double(11,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.00',
  `vlr_total_pes` double(11,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.00',
  `vlr_total_p_red` double(11,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.00',
  `vlr_desc` double(11,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.00',
  `vlr_liq` double(11,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.00',
  PRIMARY KEY (`emp`,`orc`,`nivel1`,`nivel2`,`nivel3`,`produto`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=latin1;
CREATE TABLE `buf_selec_itens` (
  `emp` smallint(3) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `orc` int(9) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `nivel1` smallint(3) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `nivel2` smallint(3) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `nivel3` smallint(3) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `produto` char(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  PRIMARY KEY (`emp`,`orc`,`nivel1`,`nivel2`,`nivel3`,`produto`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=latin1;
CREATE TABLE `buf_tp_evento` (
  `codigo` tinyint(2) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `descricao` char(30) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `ativo` char(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'S',
  `id_cadast` char(19) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `id_atual` char(19) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  PRIMARY KEY (`codigo`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=latin1;
CREATE TABLE `buf_tp_evento_estr` (
  `tp_evento` tinyint(2) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `nivel1` smallint(3) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `nivel2` smallint(3) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `nivel3` smallint(3) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  PRIMARY KEY (`tp_evento`,`nivel1`,`nivel2`,`nivel3`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=latin1;
CREATE TABLE `cad_aplicacao` (
  `Id` int(10) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `Descricao` char(50) DEFAULT '',
  PRIMARY KEY (`Id`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=latin1;
CREATE TABLE `cad_at_estoque` (
  `empresa` smallint(3) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `cod_produto` char(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `num_serie_ecf` char(40) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `data_at_estoque` date NOT NULL DEFAULT '0000-00-00',
  `qtde_at_estoque` double(12,3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.000',
  `hora_at_estoque` char(8) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `descr_produto` char(45) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `und_produto` char(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  PRIMARY KEY (`empresa`,`cod_produto`,`data_at_estoque`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=latin1;
CREATE TABLE `cad_at_estoque_exc` (
  `empresa` smallint(3) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `cod_produto` char(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `num_serie_ecf` char(40) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `data_at_estoque` date NOT NULL DEFAULT '0000-00-00',
  `qtde_at_estoque` double(12,3) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.000',
  `hora_at_estoque` char(8) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `descr_produto` char(45) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `und_produto` char(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT ''
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=latin1;
CREATE TABLE `cad_atividade` (
  `Codigo` tinyint(2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `Descricao` char(20) DEFAULT NULL,
  `Atualiza` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `Fixo` char(1) DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`Codigo`),
  KEY `Descr_at` (`Descricao`,`Atualiza`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=latin1;
CREATE TABLE `cad_banco` (
  `Empresa` smallint(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `Banco` tinyint(2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `Nome` char(15) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `Razao_Social` char(40) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `Codigo_Oficial` char(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `Numero_Agencia` char(4) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `dv_agencia` char(2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `Numero_Conta` char(10) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `Dv_Conta` char(2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `Numero_Cedente` char(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `Conta_Contabil` char(4) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `Especie_Doc` char(4) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `Codigo_1instrucao` tinyint(2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `Codigo_2instrucao` tinyint(2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `Numero_Dias_Protesto` int(2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `Numero_Contrato` char(11) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `Codigo_Carteira` smallint(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `Situacao` tinyint(2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `Dias_Cobranca` smallint(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `Saldo_Atual` double(11,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.00',
  `Saldo_Ultimo_Mes` double(11,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.00',
  `Atualiza` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `Prox_Nosso_Num` bigint(13) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `Cta_ctb` char(14) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `Tp_Cobr` char(2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `Forma_Cobr` tinyint(1) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `data_remessa` date DEFAULT NULL,
  `codigo_operacao` smallint(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `prox_num_doc` char(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'N',
  `saldo_conc_ultimo_mes` double(11,2) DEFAULT '0.00',
  `dt_ult_conciliacao` date DEFAULT NULL,
  `banco_gera_nosso_num` char(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'N',
  `ProxNoRem` int(6) DEFAULT '0',
  `Cnab` char(3) DEFAULT '400',
  `Numero_Variacao_Carteira` char(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `Modalidade_Carteira` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT '1',
  `Operacao_Remessa` char(7) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `Modalidade_Remessa` char(2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  PRIMARY KEY (`Empresa`,`Banco`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=latin1;
CREATE TABLE `cad_bomba` (
  `empresa` smallint(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `filial` tinyint(2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `no_bomba` tinyint(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `no_bico_bomba` char(2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `no_bomba_empresa` tinyint(2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `no_serie_bomba` char(15) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `marca_bomba` char(40) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `no_tanque_relac` tinyint(2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `cod_produto` char(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `sigla_produto` char(5) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `nome_produto` char(15) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `tab_preco` tinyint(1) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '1',
  `status` char(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'O',
  `encerrante` bigint(11) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `pendentes` smallint(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `encerrante_vlr` double(11,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.00',
  `modelo` varchar(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `TpMed` char(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `no_bomba_fisica` tinyint(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `no_bico_fisico` smallint(3) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `vlr_unitario_fis` double(5,3) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.000',
  `dt_hr_perda_com` datetime DEFAULT NULL,
  `dt_hr_ret_com` datetime DEFAULT NULL,
  `imp_aviso` char(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'N',
  `Desbloq` char(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'N',
  PRIMARY KEY (`empresa`,`filial`,`no_bomba_empresa`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=latin1;
CREATE TABLE `cad_bomba_exc` (
  `empresa` smallint(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `filial` tinyint(2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `no_bomba` tinyint(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `no_bico_bomba` char(2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `no_bomba_empresa` tinyint(2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `no_serie_bomba` char(15) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `marca_bomba` char(40) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `no_tanque_relac` tinyint(2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `cod_produto` char(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `sigla_produto` char(5) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `nome_produto` char(15) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `tab_preco` tinyint(1) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '1',
  `status` char(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'O',
  `encerrante` bigint(11) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `pendentes` smallint(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `encerrante_vlr` double(11,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.00',
  `modelo` varchar(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `TpMed` char(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `no_bomba_fisica` tinyint(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `no_bico_fisico` smallint(3) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0'
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=latin1;
CREATE TABLE `cad_bombalacre` (
  `Empresa` smallint(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `Filial` tinyint(2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `NumBomba` tinyint(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `NumLacre` varchar(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `Data` date NOT NULL DEFAULT '0000-00-00',

CREATE TABLE `cad_estoque` (

CREATE TABLE `contagem_estoque_proc` (

CREATE TABLE `ecf_bol_corban` (

CREATE TABLE `ecf_pagtos` (
  `Empresa` smallint(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `Filial` tinyint(2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `NoEcf` int(10) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `Tipo_Documento` int(10) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `Cro` char(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `NoCupom` int(10) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `Seq` tinyint(2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `CodFp` smallint(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `TipoFp` smallint(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `NsuSitef` char(40) DEFAULT NULL,
  `NsuHost` char(40) DEFAULT NULL,
  `ValorTotal` double(10,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.00',

  `NomeOperadora` char(30) DEFAULT NULL,

  `UltimosDigitosCartao` int(4) DEFAULT '0',
  `CodigoCarteiraDigital` tinyint(2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',

) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=latin1;

) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=latin1;
CREATE TABLE `ecf_recarga` (

  `NsuHost` char(40) DEFAULT NULL,

  `CodOperadora` smallint(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',

CREATE TABLE `empres_medicamento` (
  `empresa` smallint(3) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `Fornecedor` int(11) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',

CREATE TABLE `etq_campos_print` (
  `Seq` smallint(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',

CREATE TABLE `etq_folha_print` (

CREATE TABLE `fila_eventos_email` (

CREATE TABLE `fis_movestoque` (

  `filial` tinyint(2) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',

CREATE TABLE `survey` (
  `id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `question_id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL,
  `name` char(70) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `description` char(255) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `start_at` datetime NOT NULL,
  `end_at` datetime NOT NULL,
  `created_at` datetime NOT NULL,
  `creator_id` char(20) NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  KEY `start_at` (`start_at`),
  KEY `end_at` (`end_at`),
  KEY `question_id` (`question_id`),
  CONSTRAINT `survey_ibfk_1` FOREIGN KEY (`question_id`) REFERENCES `survey_question` (`id`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB AUTO_INCREMENT=2336 DEFAULT CHARSET=latin1;
CREATE TABLE `survey_question` (
  `id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `type` enum('NPS','LIKE_DISLIKE','CSAT') NOT NULL DEFAULT 'NPS',
  `value` char(255) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `created_at` datetime NOT NULL,
  `creator_id` char(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB AUTO_INCREMENT=2286 DEFAULT CHARSET=latin1;
CREATE TABLE `survey_user_data` (
  `id` char(36) NOT NULL,
  `survey_id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL,
  `zendesk_user_id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL,
  `viewed_at_first_time` datetime DEFAULT NULL,
  `viewed_at_last_time` datetime DEFAULT NULL,
  `viewed_at_count` int(11) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `closed_at_first_time` datetime DEFAULT NULL,
  `closed_at_last_time` datetime DEFAULT NULL,
  `closed_at_count` int(11) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
  `answered_at` datetime DEFAULT NULL,
  `answer` int(11) NOT NULL DEFAULT '-1',
  `comment` char(255) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `info_sended_at` datetime DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `survey_id` (`survey_id`,`zendesk_user_id`),
  KEY `viewed_at_first_time` (`viewed_at_first_time`),
  KEY `answered_at` (`answered_at`),
  KEY `info_sended_at` (`info_sended_at`),

CREATE TABLE `nfe` (

  `nfe_tpEmis` smallint(6) NOT NULL,
  `nfe_tpAmb` smallint(6) NOT NULL,

  `nfe_vICMS` decimal(12,2) NOT NULL,
  `nfe_BcST` decimal(12,2) NOT NULL,
  `nfe_vST` decimal(12,2) NOT NULL,
  `nfe_vProd` decimal(12,2) NOT NULL,
  `nfe_vFrete` decimal(12,2) NOT NULL,
  `nfe_vSeg` decimal(12,2) NOT NULL,
  `nfe_vDesc` decimal(12,2) NOT NULL,
  `nfe_vII` decimal(12,2) NOT NULL,
  `nfe_vIPI` decimal(12,2) NOT NULL,
  `nfe_vPIS` decimal(12,2) NOT NULL,
  `nfe_vCofins` decimal(12,2) NOT NULL,
  `nfe_vOutro` decimal(12,2) NOT NULL,
  `nfe_vNF` decimal(12,2) NOT NULL,
  `nfe_ISSvServ` decimal(12,2) NOT NULL,
  `nfe_ISSvBC` decimal(12,2) NOT NULL,
  `nfe_ISSv` decimal(12,2) NOT NULL,
  `nfe_ISSvPIS` decimal(12,2) NOT NULL,
  `nfe_ISSvCofins` decimal(12,2) NOT NULL,
  `nfe_vRetPIS` decimal(12,2) NOT NULL,
  `nfe_vRetCofins` decimal(12,2) NOT NULL,
  `nfe_vRetCSLL` decimal(12,2) NOT NULL,
  `nfe_vBcIRRF` decimal(12,2) NOT NULL,
  `nfe_vIRRF` decimal(12,2) NOT NULL,
  `nfe_vBcRetPrev` decimal(12,2) NOT NULL,

  `nfe_TrPlaca` char(8) NOT NULL,
  `nfe_TrUF` char(2) NOT NULL,
  `nfe_TrRNTC` char(12) NOT NULL,
  `nfe_TRvServ` decimal(12,2) NOT NULL,
  `nfe_TRvBcRet` decimal(12,2) NOT NULL,
  `nfe_TRpIcmsRet` decimal(12,2) NOT NULL,
  `nfe_TRvIcmsRet` decimal(12,2) NOT NULL,
  `nfe_TrCFOP` smallint(6) NOT NULL,
  `nfe_TRcMun` int(11) NOT NULL,
  `nfe_infAdicFisco` varchar(255) DEFAULT NULL,

  `nfe_UfEmbarq` char(2) NOT NULL,
  `nfe_LocEmbarq` varchar(60) NOT NULL,
  `nfe_NtEmp` varchar(17) NOT NULL,

  `nfe_dhProc` datetime NOT NULL,
  `nfe_cStat` smallint(6) NOT NULL,

  `nfe_MotDPEC` varchar(255) NOT NULL,
  `nfe_At` smallint(6) NOT NULL,
  `nfe_XML` mediumtext NOT NULL,
  `nfe_tpNF` smallint(6) NOT NULL,
  `nfe_Selec` smallint(6) DEFAULT NULL,

  `nfe_JustConting` varchar(255) NOT NULL,
  `nfe_dhConting` datetime NOT NULL,
  `nfe_hSaiEnt` datetime NOT NULL DEFAULT '1000-01-01 00:00:00',
  `nfe_XMLAutor` mediumtext,
  `nfe_XMLCancel` mediumtext,
  `nfe_XMLCancelRet` mediumtext,

  KEY `INFE1` (`ger_Interv_Cod`),
  KEY `INFE3` (`Transportador_Cod`),
  KEY `CHNFE` (`nfe_chNFe`),
  KEY `INFE2` (`ger_emp_Cod`,`nfe_lote_tpAmb`,`nfe_lote_idLote`)

  FULLTEXT KEY `nfe_distribuicao_forn_razao_social` (`nfe_distribuicao_forn_razao_social`)

CREATE TABLE `nfe_dpec` (
  `chNFe` varchar(44) NOT NULL,
```
